1. **PA**: grant or decline the separate **D89 UI-DISCLOSURE IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORITY GATE** (one dated sentence is sufficient either way). `CV-53` is closed by that act, not by code.
2. If granted, the smallest sufficient implementation is: a read-only view mounted as a child of the existing `Research` group (a route plus a `AVAILABLE_CHILD_PATHS` entry), consuming `fetchMacroNas/Iip/Cpi`; labels per `DT-1`/`DT-2` adjacent to the data (`DT-4`), visible and accessible when narrow (`DT-5`), separated from any IIPS interpretation (`DT-6`), never inventing provenance (`DT-8`); plus the two 16.17.4 one-liners (carry `code` to the caller; allow an env-supplied `baseUrl`). Acceptance: the mounted route renders real-or-refused data honestly, `502`/`400` codes reach the screen, the nav-honesty test still passes, and no dataset outside `NAS/CPI/IIP` is addressable.
3. Independent of that gate, and still authorized: the owner can use the API today (runbook §5.1); `CV-53` blocks nothing else, and nothing in `CV-32`/`CV-43`/`CV-21`/`CV-25` waits on it.
4. Live-source confirmation on a machine **with** egress is an operator step, not a repository act: `curl 'localhost:8787/api/macro/cpi'` returning `200` with `records` and `pagination.expectedRecords == actualRecords` is the shortest proof.

## 16.18 CI verification record `CV-CORR-06` (`2026-10-14`; the IRR verification workflow implemented, published and green in hosted Actions — the IPD half remains outside)

*Added under the execution-authority directive of `2026-10-14` (the routine path — an
already-authorized repository change, recorded and self-verified, without re-asking). `CV-42`
required that a CI workflow be **proposed as a PR and not added silently**: that PR is PR #98 on
this branch, the change is published, and its checks are visible on GitHub. This section records
implementation only. It does not merge anything, and it changes no governance decision. Explicitly
excluded and unchanged: the **IPD** workflow half (it needs the §20 Stage 3 `P2`/`P3` main-admission
act), `iips-platform/**`, and the `frontend/server/macro/**`, Reports, User Portfolio and
owner-context surfaces.*

**16.18.1 What changed.**

| Path | Change | Why it is shaped this way |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `.github/workflows/ci.yml` | new | `name: CI`; `on: pull_request` (all) + `on: push: branches: [main]`; `permissions: contents: read`; `concurrency` per workflow+ref with `cancel-in-progress`; `timeout-minutes: 20`; one `ubuntu-latest` job, `working-directory: frontend`, with `defaults.run.shell: bash` so no step inherits a permissive shell |
| `frontend/.ci/assert-tests.js` | new | the zero-test guard. Reads only the Vitest JSON summary path given as its argument and fails unless `numTotalTests` is an integer `> 0`, `success` is `true`, `numFailedTests` is `0`, and at least one test file is recorded; a missing or malformed report is a **failure**, never a pass; a non-integer count is a failure. Pending/todo counts are reported but not enforced, so CI does not silently change the suite's disposition |
| `frontend/package.json` | `test:ci` added | `vitest run --reporter=default --reporter=json --outputFile.json=.vitest-report.json` — the *same* invocation as `test` plus a machine-readable summary for the guard. The existing `test` script is untouched, so no local or documented behaviour changes and no dependency is introduced (the flag belongs to Vitest itself) |
| `.gitignore` | one line added | `frontend/.vitest-report.json` — CI evidence is generated, never committed |

**16.18.2 Exact commands now run automatically**, in order, each failing the job on any non-zero exit
(no `continue-on-error`, no `|| true`, no `--passWithNoTests`, no fallback step anywhere in the file):

1. `actions/checkout@v4`
2. `actions/setup-node@v4` — `node-version: '22'`, `cache: npm`, `cache-dependency-path: frontend/package-lock.json`
3. `npm ci --no-audit --no-fund` — reproducible install from `frontend/package-lock.json` (lockfile version 3, 259 packages)
4. `npm run typecheck` — the repository's established script (`tsc --noEmit`)
5. `npm run test:ci` — the complete Vitest suite
6. `node .ci/assert-tests.js .vitest-report.json` — the non-zero-discovery guard
7. `npm run build` — `tsc -b && vite build` (production build)

**Choices that are easy to get wrong, and their basis:** Node `22` and npm `10` are pinned because
they are the toolchain the recorded evidence was produced with (V3: Node v22.22.3, npm 10.9.8) —
the repository declares no `engines` and no `packageManager`, and adding a `packageManager` field
would imply Corepack and a different install path, so the runner controls both versions explicitly
instead. `npm ci --no-audit --no-fund` changes nothing about resolution; the flags only keep the log
short and stop a transient registry-metadata failure from looking like a broken build. **No secret,
token or credential is referenced** (verified: zero `secrets.` expressions in the file) because the
one GitHub-URL dependency, `iips-production-market-data` pinned to `git+https://github.com/ramkivs/iips-intelligent-portfolio-design.git#2e11fa3b…`, resolves from a **public** repository
(`gh api repos/…/ipd` → `private=false`), so a hosted runner can fetch it anonymously. **No
dependency pin was changed** to make CI pass, and none needed to be. `iips-platform/**` is
deliberately *not* added: it declares `typecheck: tsc --noEmit` and a `tsx --test` script, but that
tree is outside the established 705-test surface and folding it in here would change coverage
under the guise of tooling. The file also records, rather than fixes, that `frontend/tsconfig.json`
includes `src` + `vite.config.ts`, so the typecheck step covers the client while `server/**` is
exercised by the test step.

**16.18.3 Hosted execution — the authoritative result.**

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Repository / branch / commit | `ramkivs/iips-review-recovered` / `arena/ee3fd321-iips-review-recovered`. `git log --oneline 1b2b194..HEAD` is the authoritative list; it read **8 commits** when this section was first written (`3479110`, `0f40f1c`, `34557c6`, `ce00bbc`, `0396344`, `65e332b`, `cefb2dd` (the workflow), `395e39c` (this record)) and the documentation self-corrections that followed brought the branch to **11** at `c8b0019`. *Evidence limits, stated rather than papered over:* the raw hosted **log text** could not be read
from this sandbox (`gh run view --log` resolves to `results-receiver.actions.githubusercontent.com`,
outside this environment's network allowlist), so per-step numbers come from the Actions REST API
(job/step conclusions and timestamps) — independent of the local run — while the exact command
output is from the same commands executed locally (16.18.4). The guard step's own `success` is
positive evidence that non-zero test discovery occurred on the runner, because the step exits `1` on
a missing, malformed or zero-test report. The hosted **failure path was not exercised on purpose**:
demonstrating it would require pushing a deliberately broken commit to a PR whose checks are being
relied on, and the guard's failure behaviour is proven directly by the four negative cases below.
`G-2` (external persistence), `D-1`, `AD-20` and all three fences are unaffected: `git diff
--name-only 1b2b194..HEAD` touches no file under `frontend/server/macro/**` and no fence file.

(ii) Effectiveness is expressly tied to **durable remote publication on IRR `main`** (D88 field 5; C-1; NP-12 "*non-authoritative … recorded but not yet effective on `main`*"), hence the two-field status `DECIDED AND RECORDED; DURABLE PUBLICATION PENDING`, mirroring `D2-CM-3`. (iii) `governance/iips/DEC-D27`/`DEC-D28` fence-relief records are **not on `main`** (4 unmerged `arena/*` tips; `main` carries only 4 files under `governance/`), so they are **form** precedents and never **authority** — and `docs/integration/` is the correct home |

**16.18.7 What this result does *not* mean, and what is still outstanding.**
A green CI run proves that the established commands pass on the runner — it is not qualification, not
admission of any branch, not activation of any feature, and not production readiness; the merge of
#98 remains the Program Authority's act, and `P2` "does not authorize the merge". Outstanding in
this area: (i) **branch protection / required checks** for `main` — a workflow that runs is not a
gate that blocks, and turning it into one is a repository-settings act belonging to the
maintainer, not something to claim here; (ii) the **IPD workflow and the `CV-35` script fix**, both
requiring IPD `main` admission under §20 Stage 3; (iii) whether `iips-platform/**` should join CI, a
coverage decision to make in its own right. Next executable action: none required for the IRR half —
it is complete and green; the Program Authority's next act in this area remains #98's disposition
(and, separately, `CV-16`/`CV-24` for any IPD progress).

## 16.19 Macro capability discovery completion `CV-CORR-07` (`2026-10-14`; full ref-universe sweep in both repositories, the Windows discovery lead reconciled, candidate lineages classified — no product change, no authority exercised)

*Why this section exists: `§16.17` established the Macro state from `main` plus the three named
orphan refs. Both clones in this environment were **`--depth 1` shallow**, so "no other
implementation exists" could not have been an exhaustive claim, and `§16.17.2` did not fold in the
macro candidates that an earlier durable record had already catalogued. This section closes that
discovery obligation across **every ref in both repositories**, reconciles the user-supplied Windows
lead, and classifies each candidate. It changes no code, no dependency, no governed decision and no
fence; `D89` (UI implementation authority **NOT GRANTED**, `DT-15`), `D91`, `D-1`, `G-2` and the
three transport fences bind exactly as before.*

**16.19.1 Method — and what was actually searched (coverage rule satisfied by enumeration, not by inference).**
Both clones were un-shallowed first, which is what made the rest possible: `git fetch --unshallow` with
`+refs/heads/*`, `+refs/tags/*` and `+refs/pull/*/head` refspecs.

| Census | IRR `ramkivs/iips-review-recovered` | IPD `ramkivs/iips-production-market-data` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| refs at origin | **213** (`26` named branches, `86` `arena/*`, `98` `refs/pull/*/head`, `2` tags, `HEAD`) | **43** (`9` named branches, `23` `arena/*`, `6` PR heads, `4` tags, `HEAD`) |
| commits reachable from any ref / from `main` | **581 / 307** | **618 / 94** |
| ref tips carrying any macro-path file (pattern applied to the **path** field, never to the sha) | **130 of 211** | **40 of 42** |
| distinct macro blobs across all ref tips | **20** blobs over **8** paths | **15** blobs over **13** paths (`src/`, `docs/`, `p10/`, `frontend/`, `tests/`) |
| what `main` itself carries | all 7 admitted paths (`frontend/server/macro/**` + client trio + `api/macro.ts`) | **4 paths only**: `src/contracts/d08_macro.ts`, `src/intelligence/macro_engine.ts`, `src/ui/view_models/ui13_macro_vintage_tracker.ts`, `tests/fixtures/d08_fixtures.json` — no `frontend/**` macro file on IPD `main` |

Sweeps run, in both repositories: history-wide path census
(`git log --all --name-only` filtered to `macro|mospi`), per-ref tip tree inventory
(`git ls-tree -r --long`, which yields blob identity *and* byte size for every ref), per-path blob
identity comparison, symbol-level content sweep over every ref tip
(`git grep -l -E "wp-macro|handleMacroReadRequest|mospi|MacroSourceDisclosure"`), ancestry and
reachability checks (`merge-base`, `rev-list --count`, `for-each-ref --contains`), and PR/tag metadata
through the GitHub API. Prior evidence was read, not re-derived:
`evidence/integration/lineage-investigation/2026-10-08/` (its `supporting/raw/ipd_lineage_only_blobs.tsv`
already lists ten of these blobs, each flagged `NO` for "not on `main`") and
`evidence/integration/convergence/2026-10-07/P5-capability-sweep.md` §D ("IRR disjoint program line
(`gai-impl-canonical` / `phase13-*`, root `7325aed`, no common ancestor with `main`) … **duplicated:**
macro transport + MoSPI source; browser OIDC/Keycloak auth …"). Its IPD counts reproduce exactly today
(`618 / 94`); its IRR counts (`427 / 168`) have moved only because `main` advanced 168 → 307 commits.

*Not searched, and why:* the Windows working copy itself (no `G:` mount exists in this Linux/Arena
environment — see 16.19.2); GitHub issues/Discussions/wiki (no macro artefact lives there and the
task is repository-content bound); `refs/pull/*/merge` for every PR (only #98's exists); binary and
`p05/`-style JSON evidence bundles (path and size inventoried, not diffed line-by-line); raw Actions
log text (host-unreachable, see §16.18.3). Anything outside these two repositories — e.g. another
local clone on another machine — is outside what a remote sweep can prove.

**16.19.2 The Windows discovery lead `G:\IIPS\phase13-next-authority\frontend\server\macro` — reconciled by arithmetic, not by inspection.**
The three reported names and sizes were matched against every macro blob in both repositories. Each
reported size equals **`blob size + LF line count`** of one specific historical blob — i.e. exactly
what a Windows checkout produces for an LF-stored blob when line endings are translated on checkout
(`.gitattributes` in IRR sets `eol=lf` for eight named canonical files only and declares **no**
repository-wide eol policy, so `.ts` files follow `core.autocrlf`):

| Windows file (reported) | matching blob | blob bytes | LF lines | blob + LF | Δ |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `macro-transport.test.ts` — 12,235 | `0af644d20369f931ee7287b901eea421d357bf57` | 12,010 | 225 | **12,235** | **0** |
| `mospi-source.test.ts` — 15,503 | `20cf3ec82e2f715200d9bf9c487678199d52288c` | 15,232 | 271 | **15,503** | **0** |
| `mospi-source.ts` — 24,453 | `5ccef665e82e7bcaacf0d70a504a9f22dd3e18e9` | 23,942 | 511 | **24,453** | **0** |

Structural corroboration is independent of the sizes: the file **inventory** matches that tree exactly —
`phase13-next` / `gai-impl-canonical` carry precisely those three files under `frontend/server/macro/`
and, like the reported folder, **no `macro-transport.ts`** (on that line the transport handler lives in
`executive-transport.ts` as `handleMacroReadRequest`). The same three blob identities also appear on
IPD `arena/*` branches (cross-repo copy, 16.19.3 L-B/L-H). Current `main` versions are ruled out:
`blob + LF` there is 34,514 / 22,675 / 8,611 for the same three names, none of which is the reported size.

**Therefore: the Windows folder is a CRLF working copy of the 2026-08-21 orphan-stack macro surface
(`024ae9b` "feat: add certified macro transport", `0cc00f2` "feat: add MoSPI source adapter",
`50cfd4f` "feat: add macro research surface"), not of anything on `main`.** The files themselves were
**not inspected** — only names and sizes were supplied, and this environment cannot reach `G:`. To
convert this into content equality, either copy the three files into the workspace, or run in that
folder without transferring content:

```powershell
git -C G:\IIPS\phase13-next-authority rev-parse HEAD
git -C G:\IIPS\phase13-next-authority status --porcelain -- frontend/server/macro
(Get-Content -Raw frontend\server\macro\mospi-source.ts) -replace "`r`n","`n" | `
  ForEach-Object { (Get-FileHash -InputStream ([IO.MemoryStream]::new([Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($_))) -Algorithm SHA256).Hash }
```

Expected LF SHA-256 (from the matching blobs): `mospi-source.ts`
`7464bb7480e33015f659cd0066e472e1599803f81cda4a16b20baaf8692efd36` · `mospi-source.test.ts`
`59c4a2b9b1d5ee8ec542b637a4ea9a645d710ac5e114d96bf06ecb872983abd5` · `macro-transport.test.ts`
`467e1ada2c3fabff53acb7df248b6f2351cb562f99e5aa3a244b0e6ac2d3ff4d`. A `git status` that reports no
delta for those paths would on its own pin the checkout to the corresponding commit.

**16.19.4 The governed obligation chain — what `§16.17` lacked and what it now means.**
`D91` is established by a "constraint + disclosure act" whose commit **`727bdcb` is an IPD object**
(present in IPD, not an IRR object — verified: `git cat-file -t` fails in IRR, succeeds in IPD). Its
terms, as carried by three IRR `main` acts (`NP-08-D91-RELIEF-COMPETENCE-ESTABLISHMENT-ACT`,
`NP-08-D91-RELIEF-MECHANISM-ACT`, `NP-08-M5-D91-OUT-OF-SCOPE-DISPOSITION-ACT`): macro data is
**LIVE-only**, **EXEMPT** from the account-wide UI12 "Default data mode" preference, subject to a
**fallback prohibition**, and carries a **UI disclosure obligation on `MacroContext.tsx`** (D91 §1);
relief competence belongs to **D88** (`2dc5563` in IRR; `b607ea9` established competence);
**"No D91 relief is in force"**; `M-5` = "Relief from D91 (authority D88) for macro" and
`D91/D88_MACRO_RELIEF` **remains NOT GRANTED**; and macro **remains inside D8 Intelligence scope**
("`DEFERRED` does NOT mean `ABANDONED`"). Consequence, stated plainly: the disclosure obligation is
**operative and unmet on `main`** (the file renders nothing), while the implementations that would
discharge it sit unadmitted on L-B / L-C / L-E. That is a governance question about which authority
discharges which obligation — not an oversight to be closed by editing a component. Reusable facts for
the holder of the gate: `FreshnessBadge` (`frontend/src/components/ui/Badges.tsx`) and `EmptyState`
(`frontend/src/components/shell/ShellStates.tsx`) **already exist on `main`** and are what L-B's
rendering used.

Same act identifiers, different repositories, one unadmitted claim | **UNRESOLVED — PA decision required.** Both texts are quoted with ref and blob; neither is promoted. The PA decides whether an IPD-side act can discharge an IRR-side obligation |

LOCAL_FIXTURE only"**) and IPD `main`'s shell test forbids binding the `UI13` macro view model at all | **Status unchanged (`OPEN`, IPD owner).** The addendum strengthens the finding; it does not alter it, and it is not a licence to touch IPD |

## 16.20 Remaining open items swept against the same ref universe `CV-CORR-08` (`2026-10-14`; discovery only, applied to `CV-32`, `CV-38`, `CV-43` because the shallow clone that bounded `§16.17` bounded them too)

**16.21.2 `CV-CORR-09` — the obligation is stated conditionally, and one category error is removed.** §16.19 described "one outstanding obligation, two reusable candidates"; there are in fact **two different disclosures**, and conflating them is the root of the `XV-16` confusion: **D89 `DT-1`** fixes *provider/platform transparency* — `"Provider: Ministry of Statistics and Programme Implementation (MoSPI) · Data platform: e-Sankhyiki."`, with `DT-2` dataset labels and IIP labelled **NIC 2-digit level only** — while **D91 §1** fixes a *UI12 data-mode exemption notice* on `MacroContext.tsx` ("selecting SNAPSHOT or PIT does not switch Macro data"). `L-C`'s component is the first kind; `L-E`'s markup is unmistakably the second; neither is the other. **Both obligations attach to a rendered Macro view, and `main` has none** (`MacroContext.tsx` 5,944 B returns `children` only; neither test id occurs in `frontend/src/**`; no route, no nav entry). Related and previously unrecorded: **no D91 relief is needed in the present state** — `main` has **no client-side data-mode control at all** (UI12 exists only as `frontend/server/settings/{settings-service,settings-transport}`; zero `dataMode`/`data-mode` references in `frontend/src/**`), and the mount calls `handleMacroRequest(req, res)` while the handler's third parameter `deps: MacroRouteDeps = {}` is never supplied, so **no settings input can reach the macro path**: the LIVE-only exemption holds **structurally — and is untested**, which is the one hardening item that needs no UI authority (a server-side test that the macro response is invariant to the settings service), recorded here as a post-decision candidate and **not performed**.

**16.21.3 `XV-16` reconciled as far as evidence allows — the conflict is preserved, not resolved by inference.** The IPD-side record (on `arena/01a0a438`: `docs/D91_MACRO_LIVE_ONLY_EXEMPTION_DISCLOSURE.md`, delivery commit `727bdcb` 2026-09-15, parent `57ea3bb`, which touches **four** files — the act doc **plus** `MacroContext.tsx` +23, `MacroContext.test.tsx` +20, `frontend/server/data-mode/data-mode.test.ts` +21; authority "**D88 = A**"; "**Correct-by-addition:** D85, D86, D88, D89 and D90 records are NOT edited"; "**Phase 3 — Macro disclosure | COMPLETE (D91)**") **does not discharge** the IRR-owned obligation, on three grounds that need no reinterpretation of any act: **(a)** `NP-08-P4-GATE-Y-CORPUS-PLACEMENT-DESIGNATION` (PA, 2026-10-04) selects **C-1** — IRR is the **durable destination**, IPD the **source corpus, read-only, unmutated**, so an artifact placed in that corpus is not an instrument for an IRR obligation; **(b)** the file whose rendering matters, `frontend/src/features/research/MacroContext.tsx`, **does not exist on IPD `main`** (IPD `main` has `frontend/src/features/{evidence,executive,intelligence,portfolio}`, no `research/`, no `frontend/server/data-mode/`; the *only* data-mode tree on IPD `main` is `src/platform/data-mode` and it does not touch macro), so an IPD-side edit changes nothing the personal application renders; **(c)** `727bdcb` is reachable from **exactly one unmerged branch in either repository**, shares **no merge-base with IRR `main`** (it sits on the `L-B` lineage, using `authFetch` and `GET /api/macro?dataset=` where `main` serves `/api/macro/{dataset}` over `noAuthFetch`), and IRR's operative act states "**No D91 relief is in force**" with D89's item "historically **OPEN**; **not silently closed**". What the cited proof actually shows is correspondingly narrow: the markup is a static styled `<div data-testid="macro-data-mode-exemption-disclosure">`; the "+2 tests" are **jsdom** assertions (`findByTestId` + three `toHaveTextContent` regexes, plus a `freshness-live`-present/`freshness-snapshot`-absent check) under `globalThis.fetch = urlAwareMock(IIP_DATA)` — a mocked fetch, in a harness, on a file that is not the admitted one; the act itself records "**Browser/runtime qualification: NOT CLAIMED**", which this section adopts verbatim rather than correcting. *Candidate interpretation for the PA, not a conclusion:* because the two texts address **different obligations**, IPD's "COMPLETE (D91)" neither contradicts "not silently closed" nor closes `DT-1…DT-15`; the live authority question is the narrower one — **may an act recorded in the read-only source corpus ever modify, preserve or withdraw an obligation that IRR's acts attach to an IRR-owned file, and does D91 §1 survive while IRR has no Macro view** — escalated **unanswered** as Q2/Q3 of the packet. `XV-16` stays `UNRESOLVED`; no status was invented to close it.

## 16.22 Option E disposition and the `XV-16` determination prepared as a PA act (`CV-CORR-10`) — `2026-10-15` (recorded `2026-10-14T18:55Z` UTC)

**One consolidated decision instrument, published for ratification; no implementation, no relief, no merge.** Record `NP-08-D08-MACRO-OPTION-E-DISPOSITION-AND-XV16-AUTHORITY-DETERMINATION-01` at `docs/integration/NP-08-D08-MACRO-OPTION-E-DISPOSITION-AND-XV16-AUTHORITY-DETERMINATION-2026-10-15.md`, field 5 **`DECIDED AND RECORDED; DURABLE PUBLICATION PENDING`** and field 6 **PENDING PA RATIFICATION** — vocabulary taken from the corpus itself (`NP-13-PA-D2-C-…` row `D2-CM-3` "DECIDED … DURABLE PUBLICATION PENDING"; `IIPS_v3.0_NP12_…ACCEPTANCE_DECISION` "Until steps 3 and 4 are complete and verified, this act is **non-authoritative**… recorded but not yet effective on `main`"; `NP-08-D88-…` field 5 "effective on durable remote publication"). The packet gained only an additive forward-pointer, so there is **one** record set at **one** publication point and no competing instrument.

**16.22.1 Basis for recording a PA act without a signature (OBSERVED convention, not an assumption).** Three governing precedents were read before drafting: **(a)** AD-01 in `IIPS_v3.0_G2_2_EXISTING_CAPABILITY_CONVERGENCE_DECISION_RECORD` — "*Bounded Reports presence ratification (**PA direction Q3**)*" — establishes that an explicitly supplied PA direction, recorded by the agent, **is** the accepted instrument form and that its effect is **bounded** ("*grants no implementation, promotion, UI, runtime or end-to-end acceptance authority*"); **(b)** `NP-08-D08-MACRO-IMPLEMENTATION-AUTHORITY-DECISION-ACT` — "*every decision recorded in this act was supplied **explicitly by Ramki***"; **(c)** the NP-12 rule that an act's own merge/tree/blob/PR coordinates "*cannot be recorded inside the artifact that they contain*", which is why §8 and Appendix B defer them to this tracker. The repository's act format has **no signature field**; none was simulated. Consequently the **decisions** are attributed to the PA's `2026-10-15` direction, while the **wording** is marked `PENDING PA RATIFICATION`, because no PA confirmation of this text exists in the record. **The two axes are kept separate and neither is inflated.**

**16.22.2 Pre-disposition supersession sweep (V34) — none found, and the census is unchanged in substance.** Before recording anything, both repositories were re-fetched (`--prune --tags` plus `refs/pull/*/head` mirrors) and scanned for any later PA act, implementation-authority grant, D91 relief determination or `XV-16` resolution: **IRR `main 1b2b194`, IPD `main 4d3e1cd`, PR #98 the only open PR in either repository** (`#97`, `#96` merged earlier on `2026-10-14`; IPD's latest PR `#6` merged `2026-09-29`), **no new tag** (latest `program-v1.2.0` `2026-09-05`), and a filename-level census of all **127** IRR tree tips for `D91|RELIEF|DETERMINATION|MACRO|XV-16|OPTION-E` under `docs/`, `governance/`, `evidence/` returned **no** determination, relief-authorization or Option-E record anywhere except the packet itself (1 tip) and the fourteen `NP-08` governed acts on `main` (75–76 tips). Phrase-level search across all tips returned **zero** files containing `PENDING PA RATIFICATION` or a `RELIEF-DETERMINATION` identifier — i.e. **the third required act of the D91 mechanism still does not exist on any ref.** The ref-count movement (213 → 313 in IRR, 43 → 50 in IPD) is **entirely** this turn's fetch mirrors plus GitHub's ephemeral `refs/pull/98/merge` preview (`cd569d6`, created 17:43Z when the PR head moved); **no new authored branch exists**, and the four lineage tips (`4357e14`, `f63a9b4`, `36e433a`, IPD `ea00140`) are byte-unchanged — so the `§16.19`/`§16.21` census and the Windows identity reconciliation are **reused with verification**, not re-derived, and no test was re-run.

**16.22.3 What D-1 records (Option E), and what it does not.** Eleven clauses: the *view* deferred; obligation **preserved** under `DT-1…DT-15` and D91 §1; **deferral ≠ abandonment/satisfaction/qualification/admission/compliance** ("`DEFERRED` does NOT mean `ABANDONED`"); **no implementation authority** (`D89 §3/§19`, `DT-15` binding); `DT-1`'s string preserved verbatim with `DT-11` **NOT DETERMINED** and `DT-13` **NOT ESTABLISHED** left unresolved rather than quietly cured; all D91 requirements preserved (LIVE-only, fallback prohibition, NAS/CPI/IIP boundary, provenance, server-side transport — WP-MACRO-03 §16.1's eight constraints untouched); no synthetic fallback; **relief ungranted** and the *Relief Determination* not created, pre-authorised or deemed; six reconsideration triggers **R-1…R-6** with R-1 (the gate opening) **self-terminating** the deferral; `L-C` retained **for evaluation only** behind six verification conditions **V-C1…V-C6**. **No status advanced:** `CV-53` keeps `BLOCKED (authority — implementation gate not granted)`, `XV-15` keeps `OPEN`, `Live E2E` keeps `C — BLOCKED`.

**16.22.4 What D-2 records (`XV-16`), stated narrowly with its authority basis.** Six findings, each confined to what the instruments support: **4.1** the IPD record does establish *its own delivery* (all four hunks observed in `727bdcb`) while its "**20 passed / 0 failed**" is accepted **only as a self-reported result** — the suite was not re-executed here; **4.2** the assertions are **jsdom + mocked `globalThis.fetch`** render checks, proving neither browser behaviour (its own "NOT CLAIMED"), nor contract compatibility (`authFetch` + `?dataset=` vs `noAuthFetch` + `/api/macro/{dataset}`), nor **`DT-1` satisfaction**, because the markup is the **D91 notice, a different obligation**; **4.3** the claimed state is **absent from IRR `main`** (`2066eae2`, 5,944 B, `children` only; test id absent) and exists on **one** IPD branch; **4.4** **no** delegated authority, on placement (**C-1**: IRR durable destination / IPD read-only source), host (no `frontend/src/features/research/**` on IPD `main`) and competence (**D88** = the UI12 data-mode dimension, a power to determine requests not to originate them, §8.3) — and the IPD header's own "**D89 records are NOT edited**" is *consistent* with this; **4.5** therefore **no discharge, no conflict, no amendment**: the IRR obligation stays open with Option E as its recorded disposition, while the IPD record is **not** declared false, withdrawn or superseded, only **without operative effect on IRR**; **4.6** the four routes that *could* change the IRR disposition, each named with its actor. **4.7 preserves the residue deliberately:** the **general** question — whether any source-corpus record could ever bind an IRR obligation — is recorded **UNRESOLVED (no governing authority located in the verified scope)**, so `XV-16` is **not** advanced; its status line stays as written, now carrying the narrow determination.

Earlier 13-route transport probes (V22, §16.17) are reused because no server/client file changed. The current full suite was rerun after the CSS change.

**16.27.5 Separate blockers and non-effects.** Live MoSPI retrieval remains **C — BLOCKED** (the three browser requests returned 502 in this egress-constrained environment); no live-source E2E passed and no synthetic fallback was used. Prior V-C5’s D91 §1 notice is a separate, not-accepted obligation for any broader Macro surface; D91/D88 relief remains NOT GRANTED. The API client still discards the response-body `code`; the `code`/dataset passthrough and `deps`/config injection items remain incomplete/fenced under §§16.17.4 and 16.19.6 and are not authorized by the current implementation act §5. The old tracker requirement for HTTP `502`/`400` status text was checked as such; it does not establish response-body-code passthrough. Attribution remains NOT DETERMINED, entitlement `B — PARTIALLY ESTABLISHED`, GODL applicability and M-3 NOT ESTABLISHED, production NOT AUTHORIZED; no qualification or admission is inferred. **CV-24 and CV-38 are untouched; no other register status moves.**

**16.27.6 Status movement.** `CV-53` moves from `BLOCKED (authority)` to `COMPLETE/ACCEPTED` **only for the D89 display contract**. The register remains 35 items; complete/accepted becomes 12, blocked-active becomes 2 and active outstanding work becomes 16. CV-50 stays terminal; CV-51/CV-52 remain blocked; CV-24/CV-38, `XV-15`, `XV-16`, Live E2E, and all legal/qualification/admission/production states remain unchanged. The complete D89 Product Acceptance disposition and criterion matrix are in `docs/integration/CV-53-D89-MACRO-PRODUCT-ACCEPTANCE-RECORD-2026-10-15.md`.

## 17 Executive Convergence View (`2026-10-14`; grounds §§18–20; derived from §16.3 V1–V20 and §16.4)

*Read this first, then §18 (capability matrix — one row per capability, the exact 17 fixed columns), §19 (every outstanding register item in one place) and §20 (the ordered progression). These four sections add no decision, lift no fence, authorize no implementation and duplicate no register text: they re-express §16 in the requested structure, sharpened by `CV-CORR-02` (§16.14).*

**CV-53 addendum (`2026-10-15`; `CV-CORR-15`, §16.27).** The D89 Macro disclosure display contract is now accepted within that scope. PR #103 supplies the mounted `/research/macro` view on `main`; the Macro-route-only responsive correction, acceptance record and tracker update are published on the session branch for review in PR #104, pending `main`. This does not change the original `2026-10-14` snapshot for other capabilities, and it does not establish live-source E2E, D91 V-C5 acceptance, API-client residual completion, Macro qualification/admission or production.


**Working and accepted today (protected — do not redo).** The application runs end-to-end as a single-owner, credential-free workbench: executive dashboard with its certified DTO; the portfolio workspace over the certified reference portfolio; company, cross-sector, decision-matrix and engine-registry surfaces over the 13 certified sector engines; the governed screener (POST-only, 13 server-derived members, `422` against client-supplied membership); evidence landing, explorer and replay at `:id`; watchlists, collaboration and settings durable on the Class-C append-only journal; the owner-context carrier with its fail-closed `503`; the `G-2` §5 user-portfolio HTTP contract, port, `D-2` §8 translation boundary and the two read surfaces with their tests; the PIT read seam answering `/api/pit/market-data` against the pinned IPD package; the IPD data plane and offline shell; `REG-01`…`REG-13`; and the `program-v1.2.0` tag plus GitHub Release. Executed here: `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors, `64` files / `703` tests passing, `npm run build` `256.73 kB`, `13/13` live route probes `200`, journal append observed on disk, IPD `542/542` — §16.4.1 (CV-01…CV-11) on evidence V2–V8, V12–V14.

**Implemented but not integrated or wired.** (1) The `G-2` durable portfolio line: a complete, remotely verified non-production implementation exists — contract, port, translation boundary, G24 HTTP adapter, transport, `59` tests — but it is reachable **only** from `origin/arena/a2df3b85-iips-review-recovered`, `7` ahead and **`149` behind** `main`, and its own record §8.5 withholds main admission (V16/V17); `ipdUserPortfolioAdapter.ts` and `contractStub.ts` do not exist on `main`. (2) `frontend/server/d115-runtime.ts`: runtime `CompanyId` boundary with tests, referenced by nothing but its own test (V19, CV-43). (3) `iips-platform` snapshots and `WorkflowRuntime`: consumed by platform code and one regression test only, never by a product route (V19). (4) IPD `main` has **no HTTP server at all** and `0` files under `src/persistence` (V15/V18), so nothing on IPD `main` can serve a durable portfolio store. (5) Macro/D08 and the PIT seam are API-only: no UI feature directory exists for `macro`, `pit`, `reports` or a real `intelligence` surface (V19).

**Incomplete, data-limited or awaiting validation.** Six nav destinations land on "not yet built" placeholders (`/research`, `/research/sector/:id`, `/evidence/snapshots`, `/evidence/replay` landing, `/intelligence/*`, wildcard) while the sidebar promises capability — CV-41. Owner-scoped writes can only address the `13`-row governed universe (real ISINs answer `404 governed-row-not-found`) and reference analytics are a frozen `SNAPSHOT` — CV-22/XV-9. IPD’s broker-ingestion `PortfolioStore` is in-memory, so nothing survives a restart — CV-39/V15. The journal’s separate-process restart proof is off-`main` — CV-38/V17. IPD’s `npm test` can report `0` tests and exit `0` — CV-35. **Neither repository carries a CI workflow**, so every "tests pass" statement in this program is local-execution evidence — CV-42 (new). The runtime pin is reachable through exactly one branch ref — CV-25. Documentation still contradicts the shipped release state, and the IPD XLSX tracker reads `59/59 NOT STARTED` against `evidence/p13…p17` — CV-33/CV-36.

**Decisions and authorities still outstanding (PA-owned; nothing else can substitute).** (1) Whether to authorize the **qualification** act for the already-implemented, unadmitted durable line at current `main` — alone, or together with a bounded residual implementation act — or to defer it with a dated reason; **"admit as-is" is not offered**, because `D-1` and the implementation record §8.5 prohibit admission and it remains a separate later act resting on recorded qualification. (2) Designate (or decline) the **non-authentication ownership source** the `503` user-portfolio seam waits on — the transport states it does not authenticate, synthesize a principal, pass credentials upstream or infer a tenant. (3) Within IPD ownership, which durable storage technology satisfies `G-2` §3, and how §3’s owner-identity, isolation, auditability and durability requirements are read for a single-owner non-production application (XV-13) — the substance of §3 is not waivable. (4) Reports: `REG-11` Option C stands until an own disposition act names an ownership source, contract shape and qualification (`R1`/`R2`/`R3`/`F-7`). (5) Governed-universe scope for owner data (CV-22); `Gate A` necessity now that its only consumer was removed (CV-23); `F6_INVENTORY_AUTHORIZED` and `TD-7b` (CV-24); the pin-durability act (CV-25); the `D115` disposition (CV-43).

**Blockers specific to User Portfolio.** The absence of an authorized qualification act for the durable line (`G-2` §9’s withhold gave way only to the 2026-10-07 non-production implementation, V16), and — strictly after it — a separate admission act, since `D-1`’s standing prohibition on G24 main admission and the implementation record §8.5 are lifted only by that act and never by qualification, planning or a passing suite; the deferred live-IdP and per-user-credential acts that keep the seam at `503` (record §8.1–§8.2); no durable sink on IPD `main` (V15/V18); and the `149`-commit staleness of the only branch carrying the adapter (V17). One path inside this chain is **closed, not pending**: `P-4`→`P-6`→`P-8` exists solely to satisfy the G24 store’s OIDC-verified credential binding, which `H-18` and `GC-P2-SCOPE-CORR-01` exclude (CV-50) — nothing else waits on it.

**Blockers specific to Reports.** Its own `REG-11` Option C deferral; no ownership source (`R1`) and no contract shape (`R2`); consumer admission (`R3`) and qualification (`F-7`, including restart durability) unmet; and the unresolved `H-4`/`H-16` `D-1`↔PR #42 conflict, which only PA errata can settle. `G-2` §8 excludes Reports from the portfolio decision, so no portfolio outcome unblocks it — and no Reports outcome blocks the portfolio (CV-21/CV-51).

**Issues that independently affect other capabilities** (each actionable without any portfolio or Reports decision): pin durability CV-25; IPD false-green runner CV-35 and the absent CI in both repositories CV-42; governed-universe limits CV-22; documentation and tracker contradictions CV-33/CV-34/CV-36/CV-37; nav honesty CV-41; runbook and start command CV-30/CV-31; off-`main` restart proof CV-38; `D115` disposition CV-43.

**Next authorized action, and what proceeds in parallel.** Immediately authorized, no decision needed: **§20 Stage 1 (runbook + start command), in parallel with Stage 2 (pin-durability protection) and Stage 3 (validation-integrity and documentation closure)** — that is the whole distance between "verified repository" and "usable application" for everything already working. The portfolio chain’s next act is a **decision, not work**: §20 Stage 4. Reports (§20 Stage 6), universe coverage (Stage 7) and the hygiene stages run alongside it and are not queued behind it. In §18 the only rows whose release-readiness depends on the portfolio decision are 30–33, on Reports 35–37, and on the universe decision 41; every other row is independent — no workstream is, or may be described as, the sole blocker to convergence.


## 18 Capability Convergence Matrix (exact 17-column structure; one row per capability or cross-repository workstream)

**How to read.** Every cell judges **one dimension** independently and is never carried over from a neighbouring cell: implementation is not proof of integration, wiring, validation, acceptance, qualification or release readiness, and a passing automated suite is not product E2E or formal acceptance. Work-item detail stays in the register (§16.4); rows reference `CV-*`/`XV-*` IDs instead of restating it. `V1`…`V44` are the executed checks recorded in §§16.3 and 16.14–16.27 and are the primary anchors; `NA` is used only where the dimension genuinely cannot apply.

**Status legend.** Workbook vocabulary: `V` VERIFIED (executed or directly inspected here) · `I` IMPLEMENTED · `P` PARTIAL · `NI` NOT INTEGRATED (exists, no consumer on the authoritative ref) · `NW` NOT WIRED (no route or UI) · `NP` NOT PERSISTED · `VP` VALIDATION PENDING · `EP` E2E PENDING · `EM` EVIDENCE MISSING · `AP` AUTHORITY PENDING · `BL` BLOCKED · `UR` UNRESOLVED · `NA` NOT APPLICABLE · `OOS` OUT OF SCOPE. Extra values kept distinct because the vocabulary alone would merge materially different states: `DEFERRED` (a dated decision says so) · `NOT SATISFIED` (a named bar exists and is not met) · `NOT ESTABLISHED` (no authorized determination/evidence exists) · `NOT AUTHORIZED` / `AUTHORITY WITHHELD` (an express prohibition in a governed record) · `RECOVERY UNPROVEN`, written `VP — … off-main` where a proof exists but not on `main` · `SOURCE-REPORTED` (asserted by a record this session did not re-execute — e.g. the Windows-only shell acceptance, the `G24` `84/84` live suite, the v1.1 certificates). A `SOURCE-REPORTED` cell must not be re-labelled `V` without executing it.

**Row-level notes.** (a) Rows 30/31/34 concern the `503` fences: those are *correct* behaviour, so they read `V` on engineering dimensions and `BL`/`P` only where live owner data or admission is meant. (b) Rows 32, 33 and 36 are the rows whose favourable cells are **branch-scoped**; each names the ref so nobody mistakes them for `main` state. (c) Row 23 is a *non*-capability: `D-2` Option B withholds a universal identity authority, so the honest value is `NA`, not an open gap. Rows 40 and 44 likewise record exclusions (`G-2` §7; `H-18`) rather than unfinished work. (d) `Recovery` is `NA` wherever a capability holds no state that a restart could lose. (e) `IPD path` anchors are prefixed `ipd:`; all other paths are IRR-relative from the repository root.

**Priority key.** `P1` the product is not coherent or usable without it · `P2` integrity, durability or trustworthiness of the result · `P3` hygiene. Priority is not authority: every row names its governing record, and `NA` under Next Action means this plan may not direct work there.


## 20 Ordered Convergence Progression (dependency-ordered; no stage blocks an unrelated capability)

**Rule.** Stages 1–3 and 7 are **authorized now**; Stages 4–6 are **decision gates** whose planning is authorized while their implementation is not — planning a stage never authorizes building it, and the only authority source is the record named in "Authority Required". The one ordering constraint the evidence supports is `Stage 4 → Stage 5`, and inside Stage 5 the order is fixed too: implementation (only if authorized) → qualification → admission by its own act; a qualified-but-unadmitted line is a legitimate end state for this cycle. Nothing makes Reports, universe coverage, pin durability, CI, the runbook or documentation wait behind the portfolio decision, so they run in parallel; §16.5’s four chains, not a single critical path, are the real shape.

| Order | Stage / Work Package | Intended Outcome | Included Work-Item IDs | Entry Conditions / Dependencies | Authority Required | Immediate Actions | Parallel Work | Fences to Preserve | Exit Criteria | Required Evidence | Next Stage Unlocked |

**Stage-to-package cross-check.** `8` stages map onto the register’s `WP-1`, `WP-2`, `WP-3`, `WP-4` (Q1/Q2/Q3 plus the ownership-source designation), `WP-4b`, `WP-5`, `WP-6`: `WP-4b` is absorbed into Stage 4 because V16 showed its intended output already exists, and Stage 5/6 split `WP-5` by capability — which is why §16.7 lists `7` packages while §20 lists `8` stages. No stage re-runs completed evidence; each names the record that would have to change before it could move.
